{"lang_code":"en","pos":"adj","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"A projection operator is idempotent.","type":"example"},{"text":"An HTTP request using the PUT verb should be idempotent, so that it can be sent to the server any number of times with the same end result.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["(said of a function) Such that, when performed multiple times on the same subject, it has no further effect on its subject after the first time it is performed."],"tags":["not-comparable"],"topics":["computing","engineering","mathematics","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Every finite semigroup has an idempotent element.","type":"example"},{"text":"Every group has a unique idempotent element: namely, its identity element.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["(said of an element of an algebraic structure with a binary operation, such as a group or semigroup) Such that, when it operates on itself, the result is equal to itself."],"tags":["not-comparable"],"topics":["mathematics","sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Since the AND logical operator is commutative, associative, and idempotent, it distributes with respect to itself.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["(said of a binary operation) Such that all of the distinct elements it can operate on are idempotent (in the sense given just above)."],"tags":["not-comparable"],"topics":["mathematics","sciences"]},{"glosses":["(said of an algebraic structure) Having an idempotent operation (in the sense given above)."],"tags":["not-comparable"],"topics":["mathematics","sciences"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˌaɪ.dəmˈpoʊ.tənt/","tags":["US"]},{"ipa":"/ˌɪ.dəmˈpoʊ.tənt/","tags":["US"]},{"ipa":"/aɪˈdɛm.pə.tənt/","tags":["US"]}],"upstream_line":261587,"upstream_offset":1303928621,"upstream_record_sha256":"3334b069498cdf3970c2f9cccc49c9028edebc729a05510478257c0415607780","word":"idempotent"}
